PS> npm run type-check --prefix frontend
foreach ($url in @(
  'http://localhost/api/replication/session',
  'http://localhost/api/observer/suggestions',
  'http://localhost:3000/home',
  'http://localhost:3000/data',
  'http://localhost:3000/analysis',
  'http://localhost:3000/reports',
  'http://localhost:3000/presentations',
  'http://localhost:3000/library',
  'http://localhost:3000/settings',
  'http://localhost/api/v1/data/sources?page=1&limit=1',
  'http://localhost/api/v1/dashboard/dashboards?page=1&limit=1',
  'http://localhost/api/v1/reporting/reports?page=1&limit=1',
  'http://localhost/api/v1/presentation/presentations?page=1&limit=1',
  'http://localhost/api/v1/governance/users?page=1&limit=1',
  'http://localhost/api/v1/governance/audit?page=1&limit=1'
)) {
  try {
    if ($url -like 'http://localhost/api/v1/*') {
      if (-not $script:token) {
        $login = Invoke-RestMethod -Method Post -Uri 'http://localhost/api/v1/governance/auth/login' -ContentType 'application/json' -Body '{"email":"admin@rasid.demo","password":"Password123!"}'
        $script:token = $login.data.accessToken
        if (-not $script:token) { $script:token = $login.data.token }
      }
      $resp = Invoke-WebRequest -Headers @{ Authorization = "Bearer $script:token" } -Uri $url -Method Get
    } else {
      $resp = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $url -Method Get
    }
    $body = $resp.Content
    if ($body.Length -gt 180) { $body = $body.Substring(0,180) }
    "URL=$url STATUS=$([int]$resp.StatusCode) BODY=$body"
  } catch {
    if ($_.Exception.Response) {
      "URL=$url STATUS=$([int]$_.Exception.Response.StatusCode) ERROR=$(.Exception.Message)"
    } else {
      "URL=$url ERROR=$(.Exception.Message)"
    }
  }
}
